Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on conservation structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 698. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 221 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the conservation space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 698 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 221. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any conservation module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 176. The projective resolution has length at most 825 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
